  §5.2 hold paragraph's "runs under no request" (`[spec.1.review-citations.1]`, `[spec.1.review-fresh.1]`); no fix entry
  is recorded, so it stays open. Spec-recheck round 1 judged it below the bar as unapplied commentary and did not
  re-file it; a fixer touching that sentence for another reason corrects it in the same edit.
- DEFERRED [pkg/apis/lenny/v1alpha1/sandbox_types.go:113-118]: the `Sandbox.status.phase` doc comment lacks the input
  SPEC-4 adds. Record only; any fix goes through the Go comment plus `make generate`.
- DEFERRED [docs/api/internal.md]: the gRPC status table omits `ABORTED`. Pre-existing, for a separate finding.
- DEFERRED [schemas/lenny-adapter.proto, the LEAKED comment's drain-ledger sentence]: it may restate a rule whose home
  is §4.6.3 or §5.2. SPEC-3 does not falsify it; a reduction is a finding of its own.
- DEFERRED [pkg/controller/sandbox/podspec and cmd/lenny-adapter/main.go, a separate security finding]: the podspec
  renderer passes none of `--tls-cert-file`, `--tls-key-file` or `--tls-client-ca-file` to `lenny-adapter`, so in a
  rendered pod the adapter gRPC server runs plaintext without client authentication (`cmd/lenny-adapter/main.go:112-115`)
  and an in-pod agent can send a co-tenant's unconditional `Shutdown` (`[non-spec.6.review-security.1]`). Pre-existing:
  `unconditional_teardown` grants nothing the shipped untokened `Shutdown` did not.
- DEFERRED [implementation-checklist.md, the S23 line] (opt7 `[non-spec.1.review-applicability.1]`): "It carries the step
  id S23 rather than a position beside S7 because renumbering the earlier steps would invalidate every dependency line"
  gives a false reason, since a step can be listed at any position without renumbering, and DOCS-4's own text says it lands
  beside DOCS-2. Filed as part of the DOCS-4 ordering finding; no fix entry is recorded.

### [non-spec.1.fix-design-G3.1]
DECISION: the reservation release detaches INSIDE `ReleaseSlotReservation` (`context.WithoutCancel(ctx)` bounded by the §5.2 five-second floor, named once as a constant in the relocated **The budget:** block), so every caller in CODE-13's call-site table is covered by one statement and no row changes — BECAUSE the three post-compensation release sites sit in two packages (`BindReservedSlot`, `releaseResumeSlot` in podsession; `applySlotRetryPolicy` in sessionserver), so a caller-side detach is three restatements plus an export of `slotCleanupBudget` — ALTERNATIVES: per-caller detached ctx (hair, needs export); passing the pool figures or a budget into `ReleaseSlotReservation` (every row grows, and `rollbackClaim` holds no pool figures); moving the release into `materializeSlot`'s wrapper (needs a released flag so the outer releases skip, a second mechanism).
DECISION: the release's bound is the §5.2 floor, not the pool-scaled `slotCleanupBudget` — BECAUSE the release is one Redis script plus at most one claim delete, independent of the pool's cleanup commands, and the floor needs no per-caller plumbing.
FACT: `SlotClaimer.ReleaseSlot` with recycle=false still issues an apiserver `DeleteClaim` when the counter reaches zero, so a detached release needs a deadline; the slot counter has no per-op timeout (only `rehydrationTimeout`). EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podclaim/slotclaimer.go ReleaseSlot tail; pkg/gateway/storage/slotcounter/slotcounter.go:82
WATCHOUT: detaching inside `ReleaseSlotReservation` also detaches `ClaimSlot`'s connect-stage release and `rollbackClaim`; that is intended (a cancelled create no longer leaks the count) and is the one-home consequence, not scope creep to undo. EVIDENCE: slotbinder.go:172, start.go:3246
WATCHOUT: golangci-lint is still NON-FATAL in tier 0 (cmd/lenny-test/cmd_run.go:615 returns nil error with "WARNING (non-fatal"). The helper-landing fix is justified by code-best-practices "resolve every linter finding", not by a red gate; do not cite "tier 0 fails".

UNVERIFIED: whether staticcheck U1000 flags the `slotShutdownUntokenedEntry` var (side-effecting `mustCounter` initializer) when only its accessor is unused; the design moves the whole series to S16 to be safe. The adapter metric gate checks registration→docs only, so the S10 docs row ahead of the S16 registration passes (tests/tier11_docs/adapter_metric_catalog_test.go); no reverse gate was found, implementor should confirm at S10.

(2) Slot-keyed leak dedup under-counting two leaked reservations of one session on one pod. Rejected: the second leak needs a second unanswered reclaim or a relErr on a pod the identity gate already refuses, which is speculative. (3) The spec/10 §10.1 generation validation rejecting a stale coordinator's compensation after a Redis reset. Rejected because the tree sends no coordination_generation on any Shutdown (adapterclient/client.go:813-818), so this is a pre-existing spec-to-tree gap.

The slotBinder interface (start.go:2725-2729) declares DrainSandbox and is satisfied by *podsession.Binder (sessionserver.go:188), so accountSlotFailure's resume caller passing s.podBinder compiles. EVIDENCE: as cited
FACT: SlotClaimer.ReleaseSlot with leaked=true returns before any Redis call (slotclaimer.go:830-837). The withheld occupancy is therefore one counter unit per leaked release and is not keyed by slot, while CODE-5's tracker set is keyed by slot. SPEC-3 narrows `ReportSessionScrub` to slots that reached `running`, so the Postgres `IncrementSessionsServed` rate goes down. `DrainSandbox` runs only when the threshold is crossed.
